20TH NOVEMBER 2024 AT 10.00AM 
 
TENDER SUBMISSION DATE: THURSDAY 21ST NOVEMBER 2024 AT 9.00AM

[Page 2]
2 
 
 
INVITATION TO TENDER 
PROCURING ENTITY: TEACHERS SERVICE COMMISSION  
CONTRACT NAME AND DESCRIPTION: SUPPLY, DELIVERY AND INSTALLATION OF 
FIREWALL FOR DATA RECOVERY SITE  
 
1. The Teachers Service Commission invites sealed tenders for the for SUPPLY, DELIVERY AND 
INSTALLATION OF FIREWALL FOR DATA RECOVERY SITE - TSC/T/021/2024-2025  
 
2. Tendering will be conducted under open competitive method Open Tender using a standardized tender 
document. The tender is open to QUALIFIED AND INTERESTED BUSINESS ENTERPRISES 
OWNED BY YOUTHS ARE ENCOURAGED TO APPLY INDIVIDUALLY OR AS A JOINT 
VENTURE. 
3. Qualiﬁed and interested tenderers may obtain further information and inspect the Tender 
Documents during ofﬁce hours [8.00 a.m. to 4.00 p.m.] at the Teachers Service Commission 
Supply Chain office located at Second Floor Podium Wing. 
4. Complete tender document may be obtained by interested candidates upon payment of a non-
refundable fee of Kshs. 1,000 payable to the Teachers Service Commission at National Bank of 
Kenya Account No. 01001000905000, Harambee Avenue Branch or Bankers Cheque and present 
the bank slip to TSC Cash office for official receipt. Tender documents may be obtained 
electronically from the Website www.tsc.go.ke or www.tenders.go.ke. Tender documents 
obtained electronically will be free of charge. 
5. Tender documents may be viewed and downloaded for free from the website www.tsc.go.ke or 
www.tenders.go.ke  Tenderers who download the tender document should forward their 
particulars immediately to ddprocurement@tsc.go.ke to facilitate any further clariﬁcation or 
addendum. 
6. All Tenders must be accompanied by a Tender Securing Declaration Form.  
7. Tenders must be accompanied by the AGPO Registration Certificate ( Youth)  valid up to 
including the date of tender submission 
8. The Tenderers shall chronologically and serially paginate all pages of the tender documents 
submitted. 
9. Completed tenders must be delivered to the tender box located at Teachers Service Commission 
Podium Wing Ground floor on or before, THURSDAY 21ST NOVEMBER  2024 AT 9.00AM 
Electronic Tenders will not be permitted. 
10. Tenders will be opened immediately after the deadline date and time speciﬁed above or any 
deadline date and time speciﬁed later. Tenders will be publicly opened in the presence of the 
Tenderers' designated representatives who choose to attend at the address below. 
11. Late submission of tenders will be rejected. 
12. Prices quoted should be net inclusive of all taxes and delivery must be in Kenya Shillings and 
shall remain valid for one year (365 days) from the closing date of the tender.

[Page 3]
3 
 
13. The Tender shall remain valid for a period of 120 Calendar days. 
 
14. Teachers Service Commission reserves the right to accept or reject any tender, and to annul the         
Tendering process and reject all tenders at any time prior to contract award, without thereby 
incurring any liability to tenderers. 
15.  A tenderer who gives false information in the tender document about its qualification or who 
refuses to enter into a contract after notification of contract award shall be considered for 
debarment from participating in future public procurement. 
16.  All information provided by the tenderer pursuant to the requirements of this tender must be 
complete, current, accurate and verifiable as at the date of tender submission. If a tenderer fails to 
submit the information required, its tender will be rejected. Similarly, if the Teachers Service 
Commission is unable, after taking reasonable steps, to verify to a reasonable degree the 
information submitted pursuant to the requirements, then the tender will be rejected. 
17. The Teachers Service Commission shall have the Right to Vary Quantities at the Time of Award 
based on the available budget. 
18. The addresses referred to above are: 
(a) Address for obtaining further information and for purchasing tender documents: 
            
 
Teachers Service Commission 
 
 
 
P.O. BOX Private Bag, 00100 - Nairobi. 
            
 
Upper Hill, Kilimanjaro Road, 
            
 
TSC Building – SCMS Division  
2nd Floor-Podium Wing 
            
 
Attn. Deputy Director (SCMS) 
 
 
 
Email: ddprocuremnet@tsc.go.ke 
(b) Address for Submission of Tenders 
Commission Secretary/Chief Executive                                                                                 
Teachers Service Commission  
       P.O. BOX Private Bag, Nairobi. 
 Or Deposited in the Tender Box located at TSC Building- Podium Wing, Ground 
Floor - Upper   Hill, Kilimanjaro Road, 
 
(c) Contacts Details 
 
Deputy Director (Supply Chain Management Division) 
TSC Building 
2nd floor podium wing 
Email: ddprocuremnet@tsc.go.ke  
Telephone No: 0202892301 or 0208079033 
 
(d) Address for Opening of Tenders 
Teachers Service Commission                                                                                                                    
Upper Hill, Kilimanjaro Road,
